- **kodflow-gamedev**: SubagentStart on ^kodflow-gamedev:. One event. SubagentStart, for this plugin's agents only: the owner's feedback rules (docs/adr/feedback/.RULES.md, checked against the ADRs by hash) are handed to the agent before it proposes anything. Read-only; fails open; always says whether the digest is current, truncated or unavailable. ([kodflow/claude-marketplace](https://github.com/kodflow/claude-marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/kodflow-gamedev/hooks/hooks.json))
- **kodflow-hooks**: ConfigChange, Notification, PermissionDenied, PostToolUse, PostToolUseFailure, PreCompact, PreToolUse, SessionEnd, SessionStart, Stop, SubagentStart, SubagentStop, TaskCompleted, TaskCreated, TeammateIdle, UserPromptSubmit on Bash|Write|Edit|MultiEdit|NotebookEdit|Read|Glob|Grep|Task|A. One script per event, each a fixed sequence: gate, block, transform, observe. Hooks on the same event run in parallel, so the order lives inside the script, not in this file. Every path that is not a deliberate block exits 0. The function hooks in mod/ serve the task tools and the attribution text,… ([kodflow/claude-marketplace](https://github.com/kodflow/claude-marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/kodflow-hooks/hooks/hooks.json))
